# RH Research Program — Master Tracker (dashboard)

**Goal:** Building on `zeta-23-lean-main` (the 2026 "more than two thirds of zeta zeros are simple and on the critical line" theorem + Lean formalization), devise novel methods — advancing the existing machinery AND building wholly new machinery, up to and including a new mathematical field — toward proving or refuting RH. The 2/3 effort itself did not and provably cannot decide RH within its certificate class; the program is designed around its formalized obstructions.

**Horizon:** MONTHS, run in on/off sessions as the sponsor's usage allows, continuing until RH is decided or the program is retired. Documentation is the program's persistent brain: every session starts by reading it and ends by updating it. Refutations and dead ends are first-class results — record them as carefully as successes.

## Documentation protocol (read me first, every session)

Files and their roles:
- `STATUS.md` (this file) — always-current dashboard: phase state, live tasks, constraints, next actions. Overwrite freely; keep lean.
- `LOG.md` — append-only session journal, one entry per working session (template inside). Never edit past entries. When STATUS and LOG disagree, LOG explains why.
- `directions/<ID>-<name>.md` — one living document per research direction (see `directions/README.md` for template and lifecycle). The long-term substance accumulates HERE.
- `results/` — immutable phase outputs (maps, literature, proposals, verdicts, prospectus snapshots).
- `sources-extracted/` — plain-text pages of the three zeta PDFs; grep these, never re-parse the PDFs.
- `FETCH-LIST-ROUND<n>.md` — the sponsor's fetch list: everything the sponsor needs to fetch, with full citations, priority and reason, in one standalone file per round (current: `FETCH-LIST-ROUND8.md`, opened 2026-09-24, one optional row). **Sponsor rule (2026-09-06, binding):** fetch items live ONLY in these files — never in STATUS.md, LOG.md, direction files or other trackers, which carry at most a one-line pointer; an agent report that names a SPONSOR-FETCH item is moved into the current round file at the next harvest.

Session discipline:
- START: read STATUS.md fully; read the latest LOG.md entry; read any `directions/` file you are about to work on. Check "Live/completed background tasks" for orphaned runs and harvest their journals. Check `git log --oneline -5` for where history left off.
- DURING: new durable results go under `results/` or the relevant `directions/` file immediately (session scratchpads in /tmp die with the session).
- GIT (sponsor directive, Session 2): the repo root is `/Users/jaytyagi/Documents/Work/2026/Math/riemann` (branch `main`). Commit after every phase harvest or meaningful state change (e.g. "Phase 4 verdicts harvested", "prospectus v1"), and ALWAYS at session end. Message style: what landed + program state. Remote: `origin` = https://github.com/x67ai/riemann-rh-program (private, created by sponsor 2026-08-11). Push after every phase-harvest commit and at session end.
- END (or when the user says they're stopping, or context runs long): update STATUS.md (phases, live tasks, next actions), append a LOG.md entry, update touched `directions/` files' "Current frontier", commit.
- The persistent memory entry `rh-research-program` points here; keep it pointing here and nothing else — all content lives in these files, not in memory.

**SPONSOR STANDING ORDERS (2026-08-13 — HIGHEST PRIORITY, binding for the rest of the program):**
0. **DOCUMENT AS YOU GO — nothing may depend on the session surviving (2026-09-02, sponsor directive, TOP PRIORITY).** The sponsor's usage can run out at any moment with no warning; when it does the session is gone. Therefore: every result is written to disk the moment it exists; commits are small and frequent and always pushed; two watchdogs run from session top (`~/.claude/push-watchdog.sh` and `~/.claude/autocommit-watchdog.sh`, which commits agents' interim outputs every ten minutes on its own); this file's "Live/completed background tasks" section names every run in flight with its run id, outputs, and what to do with them on completion, written at launch; LOG.md is appended as things land, not at the close; a completed run is harvested and committed before anything new starts. A fresh session must be able to continue from disk alone, whenever usage returns. (Also RULE ONE in `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md`.)
1. **Prior-art gate.** Before committing effort to any path, verify against tier-1 online sources (arXiv, published literature, MathSciNet-visible work) that it has not already been pursued or refuted. Novelty checks are not optional and not from-memory: check online. If online access to any resource fails or is paywalled, DO NOT silently skip it — tell the sponsor, who will fetch it manually.
2. **Session-end learnings in writing.** At the end of every session — BEFORE asking the sponsor to kickstart the next one — write ALL of that session's learnings into the durable docs (LOG.md entry, STATUS.md, touched `directions/` files). A session is not closed until its learnings are on disk and committed. No knowledge may live only in a conversation.
3. **Full parallelism authorized.** Use as many parallel agents as the work warrants — take a real stab at the problem. Scale is not the constraint; correctness, novelty, and honest verification are.
   **AMENDED by the sponsor 2026-09-02 (Session 14) — SEQUENTIAL STREAMS.** Three usage-limit deaths in one day each killed ten or more agents in flight. From now on: **one workflow at a time; the next stream starts when the previous one has finished and been harvested; inside a workflow at most two agents run side by side, and only when their independence requires it (the two referees or two probes on one item).** Parallel and sequential cost the same tokens per finished unit; sequential loses at most one agent to a limit and lets whole streams complete before the wall. Standing order 3's "full parallelism" now means full *depth* — duplicated checks, adversarial passes, dual-model novelty — not width.
4. (Reaffirmed from 2026-08-11, sponsor restated 2026-08-13:) The sponsor's working thesis is that a genuine proof of RH almost certainly requires entirely new mathematics linking analysis, arithmetic geometry, and spectral ideas — a structural reason forcing every zero onto the line, far beyond any density bound. Track B/C remain the headline; density/proportion work is instruments only.
   **[AMENDED BY THE SPONSOR, 2026-09-24 22:43 IST, Session 25 — insertion-only; the text above stands as history.]** The sponsor, in chat: this "was not a mandate or directive from my side, but rather an observation … you are completely free to devise your own path/approaches, I was just letting you know that something outside the existing machinery may be needed because if existing machinery could have proved RH, it would already have … you may or may not agree with those." Consequence, binding from this line: standing order 4 and the SPONSOR DIRECTIVE of 2026-08-11 below are the sponsor's OBSERVATIONS, not orders. The program chooses its routes on merit and on its own evidence — the barrier zoo (58 proved obstructions, each naming a proof class and why it cannot decide RH) is the program's own reason for expecting new machinery, and it is a stronger reason than the general heuristic. Nothing forbids a route through existing tools in a new combination if a mechanism appears; the zoo says where not to look, not what to build. The headline/instrument distinction (Track B/C vs A) is retained as the program's own working judgment, revisable by any consolidation digest. The next digest (queue item 3) evaluates every route on merit under this amendment. Sponsor's follow-up (22:44 IST): "keeping my observation in mind, we really need to go further, wider, and deeper than what's already been done" — adopted as the digest's selection criterion: a route is funded only if it starts beyond the ground the zoo already marks as covered; "leaving the rest to you."
5. **No decisive action on fabrications (2026-08-13).** Agents hallucinate: recalled citations, "known" theorems, and numerical claims may be invented. NO decisive action (refuting a direction, committing design effort, ranking, publishing) may rest on an agent's unverified recalled claim. Load-bearing claims must be independently verified — by direct computation (Wolfram/python), by re-derivation, or against the tier-1 source itself. Adjudicators must re-derive contested mathematics, never just weigh testimony. If a needed paper is unreachable (botwall, paywall, dead link), STOP and tell the sponsor — they will fetch it; never substitute memory for the source.
6. **Grossmann before Riemann (2026-08-13).** Strategy order is binding: FIRST exhaustively check every corner of existing mathematics for a branch that fits the S1–S5 specification (the "Grossmann sweep" — Einstein found Riemannian geometry ready-made; the most embarrassing failure mode is grinding while the answer sits published). ONLY if the sweep comes up empty, construct a new branch from scratch — and there the sponsor grants free rein: build everything, from the axioms up. The completeness critic's gap list (results/completeness-critic.json) is the sweep's opening target list: arithmetic-geometry/Hodge-index (Connes–Consani), automorphic/Rankin–Selberg functoriality positivity, Gowers-uniformity input, determinantal/negative-association, Cohn–Elkies Fourier duality. **Refinement (adopted by the orchestrator 2026-09-09, on the sponsor's observation; the sponsor left the decision to the program):** in 1912 no literature connected gravitation to Riemannian geometry; Grossmann matched Einstein's REQUIREMENT (metric = field, general covariance) against a branch on its internal properties. So every Grossmann-style scout judges fit against S1–S5 only; the absence of any published connection between a branch and ζ is never evidence against it — that absence is the Grossmann condition, not a defect. A scout that discards a branch for "no RH literature" has misread the order.
7. **Dual-model novelty verification (2026-09-02, sponsor directive).** Earlier novelty claims made by one model were later refuted by another (the seed no-go's Theorems 1–3 turned out to be Winkelmann 2002; the "(T1) unrecorded" claim was Bertrand's, in print since 1997). From now on **every novelty, priority, or prior-art claim is verified twice, independently — once by the session's own model (Fable 5.1) and once by Opus 5 — each against primary sources (arXiv full text, the published paper, zbMATH), never from memory.** Disagreements are adjudicated by re-derivation against the source, not by majority. A claim checked by only one model is labeled `[novelty: single-check]` and may not appear in any external document. The sponsor's framing, verbatim in substance: this is one of the hardest problems in mathematics; no corner may be cut; the program should expect to build an entire new branch of mathematics if the existing ones prove insufficient.

9. **No shortcuts for want of a tool — install it, or ask the sponsor (sponsor, 2026-09-10 01:02 IST, Session 20).** When a step needs a tool, toolchain or resource this Mac lacks (a compiler, a runtime, a library, a package manager, an account), the session INSTALLS it itself when that is possible without the sponsor, or gives the sponsor exact copy-paste instructions when it is not (sudo, purchases, logins). Never price a step as impossible, skip it, or substitute a weaker check because something is not installed. The sponsor's words: "you can give me instructions to install these or install them yourself, just let me know if I need to do something, no shortcuts in this program, if you are facing some issue that I might be able to help you with, just let me know." Anything that needs the sponsor goes in plain English in the chat AND in the current `FETCH-LIST-ROUND<n>.md`-style action list. (Trigger: the Session-20 packaging brief had priced the Comparator run as blocked by the absence of Rust and Go; both were installed the same session.)
10. **NEVER WAIT FOR SOMEONE ELSE TO DO THE WORK (sponsor, 2026-09-29 15:40 IST, Session 35 — binding for every future session, ranks with standing order 0).** The sponsor, verbatim in substance: "if I wanted to wait for someone else to get RH done, why would I launch this program at all? … The whole point of this session is research, prove, disprove and whatever else it takes to find the conclusion for RH and settle it, no matter how many sessions or time it takes — and NEVER depending on existing work; existing work can guide us, but we are building new mathematics here." ## Hard constraints every proposal must respect (distilled)

- **DH/Epstein filter:** Davenport–Heilbronn & Epstein (class no. >1) satisfy functional equation + explicit formula + L² mean values yet violate RH ⇒ any FULL-RH route must consume Euler-product/multiplicativity input beyond L²-means. Proportion-improvement routes are exempt but must say so.
- **Formalized ceilings (in Lean, in the repo):** bandwidth-one certificate ceiling **0.6818287**; `lemmaR_tight` (two-moment rank–trace certificate exhausted; on-line double ≡ off-line pair); dimension cap d = λN; κ(λ)=1/λ+λ/3 ≥ 2/√3 ⇒ trace+Frobenius certificates cap at **2−2/√3 ≈ 0.8453 at ANY bandwidth**.

## The specification for new machinery (S1–S5, distilled from all obstructions — reuse in every future brief)

- S1 Euler-product sensitivity: consume an input DH/Epstein violate (multiplicativity at every prime beyond L²-means; Ramanujan; note Λ(n) ≥ 0 pointwise is itself such an input).
- S2 o(N)-sensitivity: must see a SINGLE off-line zero; all density/proportion methods are structurally blind to o(N) exceptions.
- S3 Multiplicity-visibility: on-line double vs off-line pair must be distinguishable (Weil-form signature is not; K_a de Branges kernels are).
- S4 A new positivity GENERATOR, not just a bigger Weil-positivity cone (algebraic: Hodge index/ampleness; analytic: reflection positivity/complete monotonicity; combinatorial: Lorentzian polynomials; probabilistic: determinantal/negative association).
- S5 Survive the named no-gos: AH world, Bombieri–Garrett 94%, parity, Conrey–Li, Λ ≥ 0, bandwidth/two-moment ceilings.

The orchestrator's seed is therefore DEAD in its own class, and with two recorded errors of the orchestrator's: the seed's Π(1 − p^{−s}) is ANTI-aligned with ζ (its zeros match ζ's worse than a prime-free baseline; the aligned choice is Π(1 + p^{−s})), and ζ's zeros are NOT a Fourier quasicrystal even under RH (Kurasov–Sarnak arXiv:2004.05678 p. 1). The archimedean-phase variant (Gonek's ζ_X) tracks ζ's zeros (mean distance 0.02 of a spacing at X = 10³) but leaves the Lee–Yang class — its analytic version acquires off-line zeros once the phase derivative turns negative (290–400 of them at X = 1000). Inverse design of U: parameter counting, no arithmetic structure. Controls: the Bohr lift of Davenport–Heilbronn's S-parts is unstable from S ⊇ {2, 3, 7}; F_{a,q}'s local factor is Lee–Yang exactly when a ≤ 2√q (Ramanujan at q); curves over F₅ are rank-one Lee–Yang restrictions exactly. The Hamburger acceptance test: sound but NOT a mechanism, and sharp — Nakamura arXiv:2008.02570 gives a function with exactly Riemann's functional equation and 31 off-line zeros below t = 45 (frequencies log(n/7)). **Group-IV candidate: 'prime channels add density' (the zero density of ξ is entirely archimedean).** Open edge named by the writer: prime channels coupled to slow archimedean inner channels — extend Theorem K or evade it. ## How to resume (fresh session checklist)

0. Follow the Documentation protocol above (STATUS → latest LOG entry → relevant directions/ files).
1. Read this file. Read `results/full-map.md` (skim `results/map-hooks.txt`) and `results/literature.md`.
2. Check whether `results/design-proposals.json` exists.
   - If yes → go to step 4.
   - If no → extract the 8 designer results from the phase-3 journal.jsonl (one `{"type":"result",...}` line per agent, labels A1:…B4:…) and save to `results/design-proposals.json`. If the journal is incomplete (session died mid-run), relaunch the design workflow from its script file (path above; it is self-contained) or reconstruct from the briefs summarized above.
3. If any designer failed/missing, re-run just that brief as a single agent.
4. Phase 4 (adversarial verify): for each proposal spawn critics — (a) obstruction check against the "Hard constraints" section above + barrier-zoo thinking (does an RH-false model satisfy all the proposal's inputs?), (b) novelty check against results/literature.md, (c) tractability check of first_theorem (are the inputs really proven? is the first step a known-hard problem in disguise?). One completeness critic across all 8 (what direction is missing?). Save verdicts to `results/verdicts.json`.
5. ~~Phase 5: synthesize prospectus~~ **RETIRED 2026-09-09 (standing order 8: no prospectus artifacts).** (Original text:) Phase 5: synthesize prospectus (rank by: soundness, novelty, tractability, payoff; keep refutation channels separate from proof channels), publish as an artifact (title ~ "RH Research Prospectus: Beyond the Two-Thirds Certificate"), save the HTML/MD source into this directory too (`prospectus.md`/`.html`). Include: the verified numerics table above, the constraint map, per-direction pages (thesis/mechanism/first theorem/milestones/Lean hook/numerics/risks), and an honest framing section (no claim of proving RH; what "success" means for each direction).
6. Wolfram numerics session id (this session, likely dead later): re-derive from the "Verified numerics" section rather than resuming the kernel.
7. Keep this file updated after each phase (flip ✅, update paths, append findings).

## Findings log (append-only)

- 2026-08-11: Phase 1+2 complete. Headline discovery: the repo formalizes its own impossibility results — the 2/3 method is *provably* saturated at every layer except its arithmetic input (bandwidth), and the single lemma chain `O1_bound → prop_PP → tr2` is where all further proportion progress must enter. Two-moment certificates cap at 0.845 at any bandwidth ⇒ proportion-1 (RH-strength) needs functionals beyond {tr, ‖·‖_F²} regardless of prime-correlation progress. PCC alone (no RH) already ⇒ 100% simple-on-line (arXiv:2503.15449) ⇒ the marginal value of *all* bandwidth->∞ pair data is exactly "100% but not RH": o(N) exceptional zeros remain invisible to every density-type method — full RH requires a qualitatively different input (DH/Epstein filter).
- 2026-08-11: Λ-channel insight for the refutation track: Rodgers–Tao Λ≥0 + RH ⟺ Λ=0 means a *certified strictly positive lower bound* on Λ is a legitimate, well-defined disproof channel — apparently unexplored as a search program (no serious counterexample-search program exists per Farmer arXiv:2211.11671).
- 2026-08-13 (PROCESS LEARNING, Session 4): single-run critic verdicts have real variance — the accidental replication run flipped A2 from swr-consensus to (computationally verified) REFUTED: run-1's killer missed a fatal derivation error a fresh killer caught, and run-1's referee had even computed the SHADOW of the error (0.74) inside the false formula without recognizing it. Protocol upgrade going forward: (a) killer verdicts on load-bearing directions get DUPLICATED (two independent killers) before a direction is ranked or funded; (b) any "survives" verdict is only as good as the deepest identity actually re-derived — critics must re-derive the central identity of a proposal, not audit around it; (c) adjudicators verify computationally (standing order 5) — this caught F1/F2 decisively and cheaply (~5 min of numerics).
